Mr. McClelland joined TEGNA in 2007, acting as assistant controller before his appointment as controller in 2015. Prior to joining TEGNA, Mr. McClelland served as vice president of financial reporting

- [Mark Cornetta](https://www.tegna.com/leadership-team/mark-cornetta/) - Mark Cornetta is serving as the interim head of sales at TEGNA, overseeing the multi-channel sales organization within TEGNA and managing the company’s annual advertising and marketing services revenue. - [Pamela Long](https://www.tegna.com/leadership-team/pamela-long/) - Pamela Long directs TEGNA’s financial operations, including budgeting, forecasting, financial reporting and capital planning. A strategic finance executive with more than 30 years of experience leading large-scale finance organizations, Ms. Long has a proven record driving enterprise value.
